namespace = POD_chargen

POD_chargen.1 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.1.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1.desc

	theme = caitiff
	left_portrait = root
	
	
	immediate = {
		play_music_cue = "mood_in_search_of_iniquity"
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1.option.a
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1.option.a.tt
		add_character_flag = POD_used_character_generator
		hidden_effect = {
			set_character_faith = faith:viahumanitatis
			
			if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = { 
						is_immortal = yes 
						has_POD_longevity_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				set_local_variable = {
					name = saved_immortal_age
					value = effective_age
				}
			}
			
			POD_remove_all_supernatural_atribute_effect = yes
			remove_character_flag = immune_to_disease
			remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_initialization

			if = {
				limit = { is_from_ruler_designer = yes }
				house = { save_scope_as = coa_target }
			}
			save_scope_as = character_being_changed
			
			if = {
				limit = {
				is_from_ruler_designer = no
				}	
				create_character = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = created_badparent
					location = scope:character_being_changed.capital_province
					age = 1200
					culture = scope:character_being_changed.culture
					faith = scope:character_being_changed.faith
					dynasty_house = scope:character_being_changed
					gender = scope:character_being_changed
					trait = vampire
					random_traits = yes
				}
						
				scope:created_badparent = {
						set_father = scope:character_being_changed.father
					set_house = scope:character_being_changed.house
				}
				
				create_character = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = created_vampirelord
					location = scope:character_being_changed.capital_province
					age = 1200
					culture = scope:character_being_changed.culture
					faith = scope:character_being_changed.faith
					dynasty = generate
					gender = male
					trait = vampire
					random_traits = yes
				}	

				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:character_being_changed.mother
					}
					create_character = {
						save_temporary_scope_as = created_vampirelord
						location = scope:character_being_changed.capital_province
						age = 1200
						culture = scope:character_being_changed.culture
						faith = scope:character_being_changed.faith
						dynasty = generate
						gender = female
						trait = vampire
						random_traits = yes
					}
					set_mother = scope:created_vampirelord
				}

				else_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:character_being_changed.father
					}
					create_character = {
						save_temporary_scope_as = created_vampirelord
						location = scope:character_being_changed.capital_province
						age = 1200
						culture = scope:character_being_changed.culture
						faith = scope:character_being_changed.faith
						dynasty = generate
						gender = male
						trait = vampire
						random_traits = yes
					}
					set_father = scope:created_vampirelord
				}
				
				set_house = scope:created_vampirelord.house 
				
				scope:created_vampirelord = {
						death = {
							death_reason = death_mysterious
						}
				}

				every_child = {
					add_to_list = child_list
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						any_in_list = {
							list = child_list
						}
					}
					every_in_list = {
						list = child_list
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:created_badparent
								scope:created_badparent = {
									is_female = yes
								}
							}
							set_mother = scope:created_badparent
						}	
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = scope:created_badparent
								scope:created_badparent = {
									is_female = no
								}
							}
							set_father = scope:created_badparent
						}			
					}
				}
				
				scope:created_badparent = {
					death = {
						death_reason = death_mysterious
					}
				}
			}
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
		}		
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1.option.b
		
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = kueijin }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.1600
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = mummy }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.1700
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wraith }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.1900
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = werewolf }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.2600
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = demon }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.2901
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = fae }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = changeling }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3100
		}
	}	
	
}

POD_chargen.2 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.2.t
	desc = POD_chargen.2.desc

	theme = caitiff
	left_portrait = root
	
	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = house_head
		add_character_flag = had_POD_character_initialization
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2.option.a

		add_trait = vampire

		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = local_var:saved_immortal_age }
				set_immortal_age = local_var:saved_immortal_age
			}
		}
	
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.3 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.710.option.o

		add_trait = kueijin

		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = local_var:saved_immortal_age }
				set_immortal_age = local_var:saved_immortal_age
			}
		}
	
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1600 }
	}

		name = POD_chargen.2.option.j

		add_trait = revenant
	
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1300 }

POD_chargen.510 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.510.t
	desc = POD_chargen.510.desc

	theme = family
	left_portrait = root
	right_portrait = scope:house_head
	
	immediate = {
			house.house_head = {
				save_scope_as = house_head
			}
			house.house_head = {
				save_scope_as = vampiresire
			}			
			house = {
				save_scope_as = house
			}	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = POD_chargen.510.option.a
		
		house.house_head = {
			save_scope_as = vampiresire
		}
			
		root = {
			save_scope_as = vampirechilde
		}			
		root = {
			save_scope_as = created_vampirechilde
		}
	
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = no
				}
			}
			set_father = scope:house_head
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = yes
					}
			}
			set_mother = scope:house_head
		}

		scope:created_vampirechilde = {
			POD_character_vampire_inherit_generation_effect = yes	
		}
		
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.610 }
		}
	}

	option = 
	{
		name = POD_chargen.510.option.b
		add_trait = vampgeneration10

		# POD_add_house_heritage_modifier_effect = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.610 }
		}	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = POD_chargen.510.option.c
		add_trait = vampgeneration7
		
		POD_create_cadet_branch_effect = yes
		POD_add_house_heritage_modifier_effect = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.610 }
		}	
	}
	
}

POD_chargen.600 = {           
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.600.n
	desc = POD_chargen.600.desc
	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = scope:created_vampirechilde
	right_portrait = scope:vampiresire

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.600.option.a
		
		remove_character_flag = chargen_choosing_sire_flag
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_remove_vampire_generation_traits_effect = yes
			#POD_remove_vampire_generation_secret_effect = yes
			POD_remove_vampire_clan_traits_effect = yes
		}
		
		POD_set_parent_effect = { PARENT = scope:vampiresire }
		POD_character_vampire_inherit_clan_effect = yes
		POD_character_vampire_inherit_generation_effect = yes
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.610
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.600.option.c
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.600.option.c.t
		
		remove_character_flag = chargen_choosing_sire_flag
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_remove_vampire_generation_traits_effect = yes
			#POD_remove_vampire_generation_secret_effect = yes
			POD_remove_vampire_clan_traits_effect = yes
		}

		set_house = scope:vampiresire.house
		POD_character_vampire_inherit_clan_effect = yes
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.601
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.600.option.b
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.600.option.b.t
	}
}

# Blood Potency
POD_chargen.620 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.620.t
	desc = POD_chargen.620.desc

	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = root

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.620.option.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_blood_potency_trigger = no
			}
			POD_character_vampire_bloodpotency_initialization_effect = yes
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.650 }
	}
}

			is_ventrue_trigger = no
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = bloodpotency0_modifier
				has_character_modifier = bloodpotency1_modifier
				AND = {
					has_animalism_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = bloodpotency2_modifier
						has_character_modifier = bloodpotency3_modifier
						has_character_modifier = bloodpotency4_modifier
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency4_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency5_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency6_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency7_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency8_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency9_modifier }		
		}
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_farmer
		}
		add_piety = major_piety_gain
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_hunter }
			}
			add_trait = hunter_1
		}		
		add_animal_lifestyle_xp = minor_lifestyle_xp		
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_animalism_trigger = yes
				has_protean_trigger = no
			}
			add_trait = protean
		}
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_animalism_trigger = no 
			}
			add_trait = animalism
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
		# Ends CharGen
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1010.option.b
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1020 }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1010.option.c
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_bloodleech
		}
		add_prowess_skill = 1
		POD_bloodpotency_increase_effect = yes
		
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_celerity_trigger = yes
				has_protean_trigger = no
			}
			add_trait = protean
		}
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_celerity_trigger = no 
			}
			add_trait = celerity
		}		
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }	
	}	
}

POD_chargen.1030 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.1030.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1030.desc

	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = root

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1030.option.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_trait = beauty_bad }
		}
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_siren
		}
		add_diplomacy_skill = 1
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT  = { has_trait = beauty_good }
			}
			add_trait = beauty_good_1
		}
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_presence_trigger = yes
				has_fortitude_trigger = no
			}
			add_trait = fortitude
		}
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_presence_trigger = no 
			}
			add_trait = presence
		}		
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_fortitude_trigger = yes
				}
			add_melancholy_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_presence_trigger = yes
				}
			add_sanguine_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1030.option.b
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_gentry
		}
		add_gold = major_gold_value
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		#POD_create_herd_effect = yes
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1030.option.b.herd
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_dominate_trigger = yes
				has_presence_trigger = no
			}
			add_trait = presence
		}
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_dominate_trigger = no 
			}
			add_trait = dominate
		}
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_dominate_trigger = yes
				}
			add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_presence_trigger = yes
				}
			add_sanguine_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
		}	
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1030.option.c
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_cultist
		}
		
		#add_diplomacy_skill = 1
		add_piety = medium_piety_gain
		
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	POD_create_herd_effect = yes
		#	POD_create_herd_effect = yes
		#}
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1030.option.c.herd
		
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1030.option.c.tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = masquerade0_modifier
			}
			POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		}
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_bloodsorcery_trigger = yes
				has_presence_trigger = no
			}
			add_trait = presence
		}
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_bloodsorcery_trigger = no 
			}
			add_trait = bloodsorcery
		}	
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_bloodsorcery_trigger = yes
				has_presence_trigger = no
			}
			add_sanguine_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_presence_trigger = yes
				has_bloodsorcery_trigger = no
			}
			add_sanguine_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}	
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_presence_trigger = yes
				has_bloodsorcery_trigger = yes
			}
			add_sanguine_lifestyle_xp = major_lifestyle_xp
		}				
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1030.option.d
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_consensualist
		}
		add_diplomacy_skill = 1
		add_piety = major_piety_gain
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1030.option.d.tt

POD_chargen.1040 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.1040.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1040.desc

	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = root

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.a
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_cauchemar
		}
		add_intrigue_skill = 2
		add_gold = minor_gold_value
		
		
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
				has_auspex_trigger = no
			}
			add_trait = auspex
		}
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_obfuscate_trigger = no 
			}
			add_trait = obfuscate
		}	
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
			}
			add_melancholy_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
		}
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_auspex_trigger = yes
			}
			add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
		}	
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.b
		
		trigger = { is_ventrue_trigger = no }
		
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_familias
		}
		add_diplomacy_skill = 1
		#Spawn herd family
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_create_herd_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:herd
				}
				scope:herd = {
					if = {
						limit = { scope:herd = {is_male = yes}}
						create_character = {
							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_young_woman_character
							save_scope_as = spouse
						}
						#Saves scope for herd automation purposes
						root = { save_scope_as = herd_owner }
						scope:spouse = {
							marry_matrilineal = scope:herd
							set_relation_herd_owner = root
							add_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
							POD_set_resonance_effect = yes

							#Saves the herdifying vampire as the herd_owner and victim as the herdmember
							save_scope_as = herdmember

							#Starts the autofeeding process by triggering the recurring feeding event.
							trigger_event = POD_herd_automation.100

						}
						root = {
							add_hook = {
								type = herd_hook
								target = scope:spouse
							}
						}
						root = {add_courtier = scope:spouse }
						create_character = {
							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_child_character
							save_scope_as = child
						}
						scope:child = {
							set_house = scope:spouse.house
							set_father = scope:herd
							set_mother = scope:spouse
						}
						root = {add_courtier = scope:child}
						create_character = {
							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_child_character
							save_scope_as = child
						}
						scope:child = {
							set_house = scope:spouse.house
							set_father = scope:herd
							set_mother = scope:spouse
						}
						root = {add_courtier = scope:child}
					}
					else = {
						create_character = {
							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_young_man_character
							save_scope_as = spouse
						}
						#Saves scope for herd automation purposes
						root = { save_scope_as = herd_owner }
						scope:spouse = {
							marry = scope:herd
							set_relation_herd_owner = root
							add_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
							POD_set_resonance_effect = yes

							#Saves the herdifying vampire as the herd_owner and victim as the herdmember
							save_scope_as = herdmember

							#Starts the autofeeding process by triggering the recurring feeding event.
							trigger_event = POD_herd_automation.100
						}
						root = {
							add_hook = {
								type = herd_hook
								target = scope:spouse
							}
						}
						root = {add_courtier = scope:spouse}
						create_character = {

							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_child_character
							save_scope_as = child
						}
						scope:child = {
							set_house = scope:spouse.house
							set_father = scope:spouse
							set_mother = scope:herd
						}
						root ={ add_courtier = scope:child}
						create_character = {
							
							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_child_character
							save_scope_as = child
						}
						scope:child = {
							set_house = scope:spouse.house
							set_father = scope:spouse
							set_mother = scope:herd
						}
						root = { add_courtier = scope:child}
					
					}
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1040.option.b.herd
		#Add herd hook to generated family
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1040.option.b.tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = masquerade0_modifier
			}
			POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		}
		add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_dominate_trigger = yes
				has_animalism_trigger = no
			}
			add_trait = animalism
		}
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_dominate_trigger = no 
			}
			add_trait = dominate
		}		
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_dominate_trigger = yes
				}
			add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.c
		trigger = {
			is_ventrue_trigger = no
		}
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_footpad
		}
		add_intrigue_skill = 3
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
				has_potence_trigger = no
			}
			add_trait = potence
		}
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
			}
			add_melancholy_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
		}
		if = { 	
			limit = {
				has_potence_trigger = yes
			}
			add_choleric_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
		}		
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_obfuscate_trigger = no 
			}
			add_trait = obfuscate
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}		
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.d
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1020 }
	}	
	

}

POD_chargen.1100 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.1100.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1100.desc

	theme = ventrue_coa
	left_portrait = root

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1100.option.a
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1110 }
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1100.option.a.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1100.option.b
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1120 }
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1100.option.b.tt
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1100.option.c
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1130 }
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1100.option.c.tt		
	}	
		
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1100.option.d
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1140 }
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1100.option.d.tt		
	}	
	
}

POD_chargen.1200 = {
	hidden = yes
	type = empty
	
	trigger = { exists = scope:herd }

	immediate = {
		scope:herd = {
			POD_herd_resonance_setup = yes
		}
	}
}


POD_chargen.1300 ={
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.1300.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1300.desc
	theme = revenant
	left_portrait = {
		character = character:505021 #Tzimisce
		animation = personality_zealous
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = character:505007 # Malkavian
		animation = paranoia
	}
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = character:505009 #Haqim
		animation = war_attacker
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = character:790179 #Helena
		animation = scheme
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = character:790568 #Anush	
		animation = war_defender
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1300.option.a
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1310 }
		}		
	}	

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1300.option.b
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1330 }
		}		
	}	

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1300.option.c
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1340 }
		}		
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1300.option.d	
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1350 }
		}		
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1300.option.e
		add_trait = celerity
		add_trait = fortitude
		add_trait = potence
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370 }
		}		
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1300.option.f
		remove_trait = revenant
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
		}			
	}		
			
}

POD_chargen.1310 ={
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.1310.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1310.desc
	theme = revenant
	left_portrait = {
		character = character:555002 # Obertus
		animation = personality_zealous
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = character:213043 # Grimaldi
		animation = paranoia
	}
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = character:555016 # Szantovich
		animation = flirtation
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = character:555006 # Bratotich
		animation = anger
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = character:96000 # Premsyl
		animation = scheme
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1310.option.a
		
		set_house = house:house_obertus
		add_trait = auspex
		add_trait = obfuscate
		add_trait = vicissitude
		add_character_flag = POD_initial_revenant_disciplines_set
		POD_character_revenant_faith_initialization_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1360 }
		}		
	}	

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1310.option.b
		set_house = house:house_grimaldi
		add_trait = celerity
		add_trait = dominate
		add_trait = fortitude
		add_character_flag = POD_initial_revenant_disciplines_set
		POD_character_revenant_faith_initialization_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1360 }
		}		
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1310.option.c
		set_house = house:house_szantovich
		add_trait = auspex
		add_trait = presence
		add_trait = vicissitude
		add_character_flag = POD_initial_revenant_disciplines_set
		POD_character_revenant_faith_initialization_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1360 }
		}		
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1310.option.d
		set_house = house:house_bratovitch
		add_trait = animalism
		add_trait = vicissitude
		add_trait = fortitude
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_trait = wrathful }
				has_trait = calm
			}
			random = {
				chance = 75
				add_trait = wrathful
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_trait = cannibal }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_secret = {
					type = secret_cannibal
				}
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = POD_initial_revenant_disciplines_set
		POD_character_revenant_faith_initialization_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1360 }
		}		
	}	

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1310.option.e
		set_house = house:house_premysl
		add_trait = auspex
		add_trait = potence
		add_trait = vicissitude
		add_character_flag = POD_initial_revenant_disciplines_set
		POD_character_revenant_faith_initialization_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1360 }
		}		
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1310.option.f
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1320 }
		}			
	}		

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1310.option.g
		add_trait = potence
		add_trait = fortitude
		add_trait = vicissitude
		add_character_flag = POD_initial_revenant_disciplines_set
		hidden_effect = {
			set_house = house:house_obertus
		}
		POD_create_cadet_branch_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370 }
		}			
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1310.option.h
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1300 }
		}			
	}			
}

POD_chargen.1360 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.1360.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1360.desc

	theme = family
	left_portrait = root
	right_portrait = scope:house_head
	
	immediate = {
			house.house_head = {
				save_scope_as = house_head
			}		
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = POD_chargen.1360.option.a		
		trigger = {
			age < scope:house_head.age
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = no
				}
			}
			set_father = scope:house_head
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = yes
					}
			}
			set_mother = scope:house_head
		}
		
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370 }
		}
	}

	option = 
	{
		name = POD_chargen.1360.option.b
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370}
		}		
	}

	option = 
	{
		name = POD_chargen.1360.option.c
		POD_create_cadet_branch_effect = yes	

		#Add house heritage modifier check here

		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370}
		}		
	}
	
#	option = 
#	{
#		name = POD_chargen.1360.option.b

#			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370 }
#		}

POD_chargen.1400 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.1400.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1400.desc

	theme = inquisition
	left_portrait = root
	
	immediate = {	
		character:791707 = {
			save_scope_as = gauthier
		}
		character:791708 = {
			save_scope_as = leopold
		}	
		character:791713 = {
			save_scope_as = maria
		}
		character:791715 = {
			save_scope_as = vittoria
		}	
		character:791723 = {
			save_scope_as = aignen
		}
		character:791725 = {
			save_scope_as = rodrigue
		}			
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.a

		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		set_house = scope:gauthier.house

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}	
		
		add_trait = truefaith
	}
			
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.b
		
		set_house = scope:rodrigue.house

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}		
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.c
		
		trigger = { is_female = yes }
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		set_house = scope:maria.house
		
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}	

		add_trait = truefaith
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.d
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		set_house = scope:aignen.house
		
		add_trait = truefaith
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.e

		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		set_house = scope:vittoria.house
		
		add_trait = truefaith
		
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}		
		
		add_trait = truefaith
		
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.f
		
		set_house = scope:leopold.house

		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		
		add_trait = truefaith
	}

		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.g

	}

		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.h

	}

		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.i

	}

POD_chargen.1410 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.1410.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1410.desc

	theme = inquisition
	left_portrait = root

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.a
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		add_trait = truefaith
		if = {
			limit = { 
				has_trait = order_member
			}
		remove_trait = order_member
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 
				has_trait = devoted
			}
		remove_trait = devoted
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_give_orison_effect = yes
		}
		dynasty = { add_dynasty_modifier = custom_lay_order_modifier }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.b

		hidden_effect = {

		}

	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.1700.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1700.desc

	theme = mummy
	
	trigger = { has_mummy_trait_trigger = no }

	on_trigger_fail = {
		POD_mummy_initialization_effect = yes
	}
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:horus
		animation = personality_vengeful
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:hemhemti
		animation = scheme
	}
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = scope:ishmael
		animation = disbelief
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = scope:cabirus	
		animation = worry
	}
	
	immediate = {		
		character:792593 = {
			save_scope_as = horus
		}
		character:792607 = {
			save_scope_as = ishmael
		}	
		character:792611 = {
			save_scope_as = hemhemti
		}
		character:792600 = {
			save_scope_as = cabirus
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1700.option.a

		add_trait = shemsuheru
		set_character_faith = faith:code_of_horus
		POD_mummy_initialization_effect = yes												 
	}
			
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1700.option.b

		add_trait = shemsuheru
		set_character_faith = faith:ishmaelite
		POD_mummy_initialization_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1700.option.c

		add_trait = banemummy
		set_character_faith = faith:faces_of_apophis
		POD_mummy_initialization_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1700.option.d

		add_trait = cabiri
		set_character_faith = faith:cabiri
		POD_mummy_initialization_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1700.option.e
		
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1800 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1700.option.f
		
		trigger = { exists = scope:character_being_changed }
		
		remove_trait = mummy
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}	
}

# Wraith
POD_chargen.1900 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.1900.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1900.desc

	theme = wraith
	left_portrait = root
	
	trigger = { 
		NOR = {
			has_trait = arcanos_embody
			has_trait = risen
			has_trait = lament
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2000
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1900.option.a
		
		add_trait = arcanos_embody
		
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2000 }
	}
			
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1900.option.b
		
		add_trait = risen
		POD_conduit_initiation_effect = yes
		
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2000 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1900.option.c
		
		add_trait = lament
		change_government = renegade_government
		set_character_faith = faith:drop_outs
		POD_wraith_initialization_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1900.option.d
		
		add_internal_flag = dangerous
		
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2000 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1900.option.e
		
		trigger = { exists = scope:character_being_changed }
		
		remove_trait = wraith
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}
}

POD_chargen.2000 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.2000.t
	desc = POD_chargen.2000.desc

	theme = wraith
	left_portrait = root
	
	trigger = { NOT = { has_trait = lament } }

	on_trigger_fail = {
		change_government = renegade_government
		set_character_faith = faith:drop_outs
		POD_wraith_initialization_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2000.option.a
		
		change_government = hierarchy_government
		custom_tooltip = hierarchy_government_modifier
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2100 }
	}
			
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2000.option.b
		
		change_government = guild_government
		custom_tooltip = guild_government_modifier
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2200 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2000.option.c
		
		change_government = far_shore_government
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2300 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2000.option.e
		
		change_government = renegade_government
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2400 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2000.option.f
		
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2500 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2000.option.g
		
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = arcanos_embody }
			remove_trait = arcanos_embody
			remove_perk = whispers_perk
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = risen }
			remove_trait = risen
			POD_remove_conduit_modifiers_effect = yes
		}
		
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1900 }
	}
}

POD_chargen.2800 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.2800.t
	desc = POD_chargen.2800.desc

	theme = witchcraft
	left_portrait = root
	
	trigger = { 
		NOR = {
			religion = { is_in_family = rf_wyrm }
			religion = { is_in_family = rf_gaia }
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		POD_assign_initial_rage_effect = yes
		POD_assign_initial_gnosis_effect = yes
		add_character_flag = immune_to_disease
		POD_set_litany_government_effect = yes

			}

# Demon
POD_chargen.2901 = {
	title = POD_demon_maintenance.10.t
	desc = POD_demon_maintenance.10.desc
	theme = demon

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = idle
	}
	
	trigger = {
		has_trait = demon
		OR = { #Niku first picks his bound land by his custom content, so skip for him
			NOT = { this = character:792736 }
			current_date >= 1230.2.2
		}
		NOR = {
			has_trait = truefallen
			has_variable = stone_prison
			has_variable = earth_bound
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2900
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_demon_maintenance.10.option.a
		trigger = { NOT = { has_trait = earthbound } }
		
		add_trait = truefallen
		
		if = {
			limit = { num_of_relation_demonic_thrall = 0 }
			random_courtier_or_guest = {
				limit = {
					religion = religion:demons_religion
					can_be_thrall = yes
				}
				alternative_limit = { can_be_thrall = yes }
				set_relation_demonic_master = root
			}
		}
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2900
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_demon_maintenance.10.option.b
		
		custom_tooltip = earthbound_artifact_choice_tt
		
		add_trait = earthbound
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2902
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_demon_maintenance.10.option.c
		
		add_trait = earthbound
		custom_tooltip = POD_demon_maintenance.10.option.c.tt
		set_variable = {
			name = earth_bound
			value = capital_county
		}
		capital_county = { 
			add_county_modifier = earthbound_modifier 
			set_variable = { name = reliquarie value = root } 
		}
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2900

POD_chargen.2900 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.2900.t
	desc = POD_chargen.2900.desc

	theme = demon
	
	trigger = { NOT = { religion = { is_in_family = rf_demons } } }

	on_trigger_fail = {
		change_government = infernal_court_government
		POD_demon_initialization_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3000
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = { 
				id = POD_chargen.3000
				months = 1 # Timing to allow game give dynast to player
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.a
		
		set_character_faith = faith:avarice	
	}
			
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.b
		
		set_character_faith = faith:envy		
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.c
		
		set_character_faith = faith:gluttony	
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.d
		
		set_character_faith = faith:lust
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.e
		
		set_character_faith = faith:pride
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.f
		
		set_character_faith = faith:sloth
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.g
		
		set_character_faith = faith:wrath
	}
	
	after = {
		change_government = infernal_court_government
		POD_demon_initialization_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3000
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = { 
				id = POD_chargen.3000
				months = 1 # Timing to allow game give dynast to player
			}
		}
	}
}

				add = 50

				}

POD_chargen.3001 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.3001.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3001.desc

	theme = demon
	
	trigger = { 
		is_from_ruler_designer = yes
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		dynasty.dynast = { set_house = scope:coa_target } # Making player dynast
		POD_demon_house_initialization_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3001.option.a
		dynasty.dynast = { set_house = scope:coa_target } # Making player dynast
		POD_demon_house_initialization_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3001.option.b
		POD_create_cadet_branch_effect = yes
		dynasty.dynast = { set_house = scope:coa_target } # Making player dynast 
		POD_demon_house_initialization_effect = yes # Need repeat three time to allow cadet branch with rd coa
	}
}

POD_chargen.3100 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.3100.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3100.desc

	theme = fae
	
	trigger = { has_kith_trigger = no }

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3700
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_kithain
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3200
			
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = real_father
				real_father = { has_trait = kithain }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = mother
				mother = { has_trait = kithain }
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_jinn
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3300

			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = mother
				mother = { has_trait = phyla }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {

			has_trait = fae
			exists = scope:character_being_changed
		}
		
		remove_trait = fae

			base = 0
		}
	}	

	option = {

POD_chargen.3700 = {
	type = character_event
	title = POD_chargen.3700.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3700.desc

	theme = fae
	
	trigger = { NOT = { religion = { is_in_family = rf_fae } } }

	on_trigger_fail = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = fae }
			POD_fae_initialization_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = changeling }
			POD_changeling_initialization_effect = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = winter_court_religion
			
		religion:winter_court_religion = {
			random_faith = {
				save_scope_as = this_faith
			}
		}
		set_character_faith = scope:this_faith
			
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = father
				real_father.religion = religion:winter_court_religion
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = mother
				mother.religion = religion:winter_court_religion
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = spring_court_religion
			
		religion:spring_court_religion = {
			random_faith = {
				save_scope_as = this_faith
			}
		}
		set_character_faith = scope:this_faith
			
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = father
				real_father.religion = religion:spring_court_religion
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				exists = mother
				mother.religion = religion:spring_court_religion
